-------- seekback: resume60.py (trace, 60 s from vout resumed)
dropped 0 per 20 s [0, 0, 0] | late 3 per 20 s [1, 1, 1] | first video render +8.4 ms drift -80330.8 ms | first audio render +1356.4 ms drift 0.0 ms | first audio DEC OUT +18.1 ms | audio − video first render 1348.0 ms

### resumecheck.py
# resumecheck.py <app log> [--clock]: the last player session. Prints (with --clock) every app [player]/[framestep] line and
# every "1g:" instrumentation line from the pause to 5 s after Play. Then, for the first 60 s of playback after Play (app
# log wall clock; VLC lines inherit the time of the last stamped app line before them):
#   dropped = "picture is too late to be displayed", late = "picture displayed late", with their "missing N ms" values,
#   the first picture after Play ("Received first picture" / first late line) and the audio output's "started" line.
import re, sys, datetime
lines = open(sys.argv[1], errors='replace').read().split('\n')
starts = [i for i, l in enumerate(lines) if '[player] play() called' in l]
lines = lines[starts[-1]:] if starts else lines
def ts(l):
    m = re.match(r'(\d+):(\d\d):(\d\d)\.(\d\d\d)\s*(AM|PM)', l.replace(' ', ' '))
    if not m: return None
    h = int(m.group(1)) % 12 + (12 if m.group(5) == 'PM' else 0)
    return h * 3600 + int(m.group(2)) * 60 + int(m.group(3)) + int(m.group(4)) / 1000
cur = None; stamped = []
for l in lines:
    t = ts(l)
    if t is not None: cur = t
    stamped.append((cur, l))
pause = next((i for i, (t, l) in enumerate(stamped) if '[player] pause (' in l), None)
play = next((i for i in range(pause + 1 if pause is not None else 0, len(stamped)) if '[player] play (' in stamped[i][1]), None)
if pause is None or play is None: print('pause/play not found', pause, play); sys.exit()
tp = stamped[play][0]
print(f'pause: {stamped[pause][1].strip()}'); print(f'play:  {stamped[play][1].strip()}  (paused {tp - stamped[pause][0]:.3f} s wall)')
if '--clock' in sys.argv:
    print('--- app + 1g lines from the pause to Play + 5 s')
    for t, l in stamped[pause:]:
        if t is not None and t > tp + 5: break
        if '1g:' in l or '[player]' in l or '[framestep]' in l or 'Stream buffering done' in l or 'ES_OUT_RESET_PCR' in l:
            print('  ' + l.strip()[:330])
end = next((i for i in range(play, len(stamped)) if stamped[i][0] is not None and stamped[i][0] > tp + 60 or '[player] dismiss' in stamped[i][1]), len(stamped))
win = [l for _, l in stamped[play:end]]
drop = [int(m.group(1)) for l in win for m in [re.search(r'too late to be displayed \(missing (-?\d+) ms\)', l)] if m]
late = [int(m.group(1)) for l in win for m in [re.search(r'picture displayed late \(missing (-?\d+) ms\)', l)] if m]
first_pic = next((k for k, l in enumerate(win) if 'Received first picture' in l), None)
started = next((k for k, l in enumerate(win) if re.search(r'\] started$', l.strip()) or l.strip() == '[DBG] started'), None)
first_bad = next((k for k, l in enumerate(win) if 'too late to be displayed' in l or 'picture displayed late' in l), None)
print(f'first 60 s after Play (log lines {play + 1}..{end}, wall {((stamped[end - 1][0] or tp) - tp):.1f} s): dropped {len(drop)} {drop[:6]}{"…" if len(drop) > 6 else ""} | shown late {len(late)} {late[:6]}')
print(f'  line offsets after Play: Received first picture {first_pic}, audio output started {started}, first late/dropped {first_bad}')
### pcrcheck.py
# pcrcheck.py <trace.json>: last session. From the vout 'paused' event to 70 s after 'resumed': per 5 s bucket of trace time,
# the demux PCR range, video/audio DEMUX OUT counts, and the last video fifo_count/fifo_size.
import json, sys, collections
ev = []
for line in open(sys.argv[1], errors='replace'):
    try: o = json.loads(line)
    except Exception: continue
    b = o['Body']; b['_t'] = int(o['Timestamp']); ev.append(b)
ev.sort(key=lambda b: b['_t'])
vid = 'video/1'
pz = max(i for i, b in enumerate(ev) if b.get('id') == vid and b.get('event') == 'paused')
rs = next((i for i in range(pz, len(ev)) if ev[i].get('id') == vid and ev[i].get('event') == 'resumed'), len(ev) - 1)
t0 = ev[pz]['_t']; tr = ev[rs]['_t']
print(f'paused at trace t0; resumed at +{(tr - t0) / 1e9:.2f} s')
B = collections.OrderedDict()
for b in ev[pz:]:
    if b['_t'] > tr + 70e9: break
    k = int((b['_t'] - t0) // 5e9) * 5
    d = B.setdefault(k, {'pcr': [], 'v': 0, 'a': 0, 'fifo': None})
    if b.get('type') == 'DEMUX' and b.get('id') == 'PCR': d['pcr'].append(int(b['pcr']) // 1000)
    if b.get('type') == 'DEMUX' and b.get('stream') == 'OUT':
        if b.get('id') == vid: d['v'] += 1
        elif str(b.get('id', '')).startswith('audio'): d['a'] += 1
    if b.get('id') == vid and 'fifo_count' in b: d['fifo'] = (b['fifo_count'], b['fifo_size'])
for k, d in B.items():
    tag = ' <resume' if k <= (tr - t0) / 1e9 < k + 5 else ''
    pr = f"{d['pcr'][0]}..{d['pcr'][-1]} ms ({len(d['pcr'])})" if d['pcr'] else '-'
    print(f'+{k:3d}s  PCR {pr:32s} demux out video {d["v"]:4d} audio {d["a"]:4d}  video fifo {d["fifo"]}{tag}')
### resume60.py
# resume60.py <trace.json> [video id]: last session. From the vout 'resumed' event (the last one), 60 s of trace time:
#   dropped = video RENDER 'toolate' events, late = video RENDER 'late' events, per 20 s;
#   first video realtime RENDER after resume (its drift), first audio realtime RENDER after resume (its drift),
#   and the audio decoder's first DEC OUT after resume, relative to the first video render.
import json, sys
vid = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 'video/1'
ev = []
for line in open(sys.argv[1], errors='replace'):
    try: o = json.loads(line)
    except Exception: continue
    b = o['Body']; b['_t'] = int(o['Timestamp']); ev.append(b)
ev.sort(key=lambda b: b['_t'])
rs = max(i for i, b in enumerate(ev) if b.get('id') == vid and b.get('event') == 'resumed')
t0 = ev[rs]['_t']; win = [b for b in ev[rs:] if b['_t'] <= t0 + 60e9]
aid = next((b['id'] for b in ev if b.get('type') == 'DEC' and str(b.get('id', '')).startswith('audio')), None)
tl = [b['_t'] for b in win if b.get('id') == vid and b.get('event') == 'toolate']
lt = [b['_t'] for b in win if b.get('id') == vid and b.get('event') == 'late']
per = lambda ts: [sum(1 for x in ts if t0 + k * 20e9 <= x < t0 + (k + 1) * 20e9) for k in range(3)]
fv = next((b for b in win if b.get('id') == vid and b.get('type') == 'RENDER' and b.get('mode') == 'realtime'), None)
fa = next((b for b in win if b.get('id') == aid and b.get('type') == 'RENDER' and b.get('mode') == 'realtime'), None)
ao = next((b for b in win if b.get('id') == aid and b.get('type') == 'DEC' and b.get('stream') == 'OUT'), None)
ms = lambda b: None if b is None else round((b['_t'] - t0) / 1e6, 1)
dr = lambda b: None if b is None else round(int(b.get('drift', 0)) / 1e6, 1)
print(f'dropped {len(tl)} per 20 s {per(tl)} | late {len(lt)} per 20 s {per(lt)} | first video render +{ms(fv)} ms drift {dr(fv)} ms | '
      f'first audio render +{ms(fa)} ms drift {dr(fa)} ms | first audio DEC OUT +{ms(ao)} ms | audio − video first render {None if fa is None or fv is None else round((fa["_t"] - fv["_t"]) / 1e6, 1)} ms')
